_r0(_gv0,"\108\111\097\100\115\116\114\105\110\103")~=_ls0)then return true end return false end end local function HCh(b85)if b85=="0"then return{}end local alpha="!#$%&()*+,-./0123456789:;<=>?@ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ^_`abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"local map={}for i=1,85 do map[Ot(alpha,i)]=i-1 end local pad=Ot(b85,1)-48 local len=#b85 local raw={}local n=0 for i=2,len,5 do local b1,b2,b3,b4,b5=Ot(b85,i,i+4)local val=map[b1]*52200625+map[b2]*614125+map[b3]*7225+map[b4]*85+map[b5]raw[n+1]=Da(val/16777216)%256 raw[n+2]=Da(val/65536)%256 raw[n+3]=Da(val/256)%256 raw[n+4]=val%256 n=n+4 end for _=1,pad do raw[n]=nil;n=n-1 end local s0=(((163316+200122)+1461959563)+wBA)%4294967296 local s1=((229275+62324)+249372)local s2=((772476+569532)+1796033351)local s3=(iQ(((195851+72849)+1310041488),mm)+wBA)%4294967296 for i=1,n do s0=(s0+s1)%4294967296 s3=Jkq(iQ(s3,s0),7)s2=(s2+s3)%4294967296 s1=Jkq(iQ(s1,s2),8)local cb=raw[i]local kb=iQ(Da(s0/16777216),Da(s2/65536)%256)raw[i]=iQ(cb,kb)s2=iQ(s2,cb*65537+(i%251))end if n<5 then return{}end local body_len=n-4 local chk=2166136261 for i=1,body_len do chk=(iQ(chk,raw[i])*65521+97)%4294967296 end local exp_chk=raw[body_len+1]+raw[body_len+2]*256+raw[body_len+3]*65536+raw[body_len+4]*16777216 if chk~=exp_chk then return{}end local pos=1 local comp_flag=raw[pos];pos=pos+1 if comp_flag==1 then local uncomp_len=raw[pos]+raw[pos+1]*256+raw[pos+2]*65536+raw[pos+3]*16777216 pos=pos+4 local lzout={}local op_pos=0 local ip=pos while ip<=body_len do local tok=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 local lit_len=Da(tok/16)if lit_len==15 then while ip<=body_len do local sb=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 lit_len=lit_len+sb if sb~=255 then break end end end for _=1,lit_len do op_pos=op_pos+1;lzout[op_pos]=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 end if ip>body_len then break end local off=raw[ip]+raw[ip+1]*256;ip=ip+2 local mlen=(tok%16)+4 if(tok%16)==15 then while ip<=body_len do local sb=raw[ip];ip=ip+1 mlen=mlen+sb if sb~=255 then break end end end local mpos=op_pos-off for _=1,mlen do op_pos=op_pos+1;mpos=mpos+1 lzout[op_pos]=lzout[mpos]end end if op_pos~=uncomp_len then return{}end raw=lzout pos=1 end local function ru8()local x=raw[pos];pos=pos+1;return x end local function ru16()local x=raw[pos]+raw[pos+1]*256;pos=pos+2;return x end local function ru32()local x=raw[pos]+raw[pos+1]*256+raw[pos+2]*65536+raw[pos+3]*16777216 pos=pos+4;return x end local function rf64()local b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6,b7,b8=raw[pos],raw[pos+1],raw[pos+2],raw[pos+3],raw[pos+4],raw[pos+5],raw[pos+6],raw[pos+7]pos=pos+8 local lo=b1+b2*256+b3*65536+b4*16777216 local sign=(b8>=128)and-1 or 1 local exp=(b8%128)*16+Da(b7/16)local top20=(b7%16)*65536+b6*256+b5 local mant=top20*4294967296+lo if exp==0 then if mant==0 then return sign*0 end return sign*GWl(mant,-1074)elseif exp==2047 then return(mant==0)and(sign*math.huge)or(0/0)end return sign*GWl(mant+4503599627370496,exp-1075)end local function rstr(slen)if slen==0 then return""end local chunks={}local ci=0 local stop=pos+slen-1 for s=pos,stop,1024 do local e=s+1023 if e>stop then e=stop end ci=ci+1 chunks[ci]=jx(XPv(raw,s,e))end pos=stop+1 return(ci==1)and chunks[1]or I9(chunks)end local num_protos=ru32()local plist={}for pi=1,num_protos do local nparams=ru8()local is_va=ru8()~=0 local max_stk=ru8()local nuv=ru16()local uvs={}for i=1,nuv do uvs[i]=ru16()end local nk=ru32()local ks={}local kenc={}local has_kenc=false for i=1,nk do local tag=ru8()if tag==0 then ks[i]=nil elseif tag==1 then ks[i]=(ru8()~=0)elseif tag==2 then ks[i]=ru32();kenc[i]=2;has_kenc=true elseif tag==3 then ks[i]=rf64()else ks[i]=rstr(ru32())if#ks[i]>0 then kenc[i]=(tag==5)and 3 or 1;has_kenc=true end end end local nch=ru16()local ch={}for i=1,nch do ch[i]=ru16()+1 end local ncode=ru32()local code={}local cbase=pos-1 local o0=cbase+2*ncode local o1=cbase+3*ncode local o2=cbase+0*ncode local o3=cbase+1*ncode for i=1,ncode do code[i]=raw[o0+i]+raw[o1+i]*256+(raw[o2+i]+raw[o3+i]*256)*65536 end pos=pos+ncode*4 plist[pi]={nparams,is_va,max_stk,uvs,ks,ch,code,has_kenc and kenc or nil,pi}end return plist end local end end return R4(J38[1],nil)end)()
